- **bec-code-reviewer**: 检查正确性、并发、资源生命周期及回归覆盖。沿调用链确认后再报告，按影响排序并给出准确文件和触发条件；默认不修改业务代码。 ([bovinphang/backend-craft](https://github.com/bovinphang/backend-craft/blob/HEAD/localized/zh-CN/agents/bec-code-reviewer.md) · ★1)

- **bec-debugger**: 复现故障、保留原始证据，每次提出一个可证伪假设。先做区分实验，再实施最小修复，补回归并重验原触发条件。 ([bovinphang/backend-craft](https://github.com/bovinphang/backend-craft/blob/HEAD/localized/zh-CN/agents/bec-debugger.md) · ★1)
- **bec-debugger**: Reproduce the failure, preserve the original evidence and form one falsifiable hypothesis at a time. ([bovinphang/backend-craft](https://github.com/bovinphang/backend-craft/blob/HEAD/agents/bec-debugger.md) · ★1)

- **bec-doc-updater**: Check manifests, actual command entry points, schemas, configuration and CI as facts. Update examples, prerequisites, failure modes and migration notes together. - **bec-performance-optimizer**: Establish a representative workload and baseline before changing code. Measure latency distributions, throughput, allocation and saturation. - **beekeeper**: The BEEKEEPER 🐝 (formerly supervisor) — the gardener's per-feature pipeline warden, one per feature the gardener hands off. Owns the flow from launch to result: extracts the next agent's context, selects and dispatches it, watches the orchard lifecycle, verifies death and timeout, and fires the groundskeeper close in reverse creation order. It choreographs — it never authors the work, never judges it (that is Valve), and never kills (Decision-081). It releases what it created; the gardener releases it. - **beforeafter**: **Method.** - Lock-in on a tracked phase: phi = 2*pi*integral(2.089 * v_ref) dt, with v_ref = 1 s mean of v_actual.
